rx.try_recv() {\n            latest = Some(o);\n        }\n        self.pending_completion_resolve_rx = Some(rx);\n        let Some(outcome) = latest else {\n            return;\n        };\n        self.pending_completion_resolve_token = None;\n        // Fill the meta entry with the resolved fields. Don't\n        // touch fields the host already extracted (label,\n        // insert_text, etc.) -- only documentation +\n        // additionalTextEdits + detail are typically lazy.\n        let Some(meta) = self\n            .insert_completion_lsp_meta\n            .get_mut(outcome.meta_index)\n        else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let resolved = outcome.resolved;\n        if let Some(d) = resolved.documentation.as_ref() {\n            let body = match d {\n                lsp_types::Documentation::String(s) => s.clone(),\n                lsp_types::Documentation::MarkupContent(mc) => mc.value.clone(),\n            };\n            meta.documentation = Some(body);\n        }\n        if let Some(detail) = resolved.detail.clone() {\n            meta.detail = Some(detail);\n        }\n        if let Some(adds) = resolved.additional_text_edits.clone() {\n            meta.additional_text_edits = adds;\n        }\n        if let Some(cmd) = resolved.command.clone() {\n            meta.command = Some(cmd);\n        }\n        meta.resolved = true;\n        // Refresh the docs popup body when this resolve was\n        // for the currently-focused candidate.\n        let Some(state) = self.insert_completion.as_mut() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let Some(doc_popup) = state.doc_popup.as_mut() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        // Confirm the popup is still focused on the same item\n        // -- a fast re-navigate could have moved focus away.\n        let Some(cand) = state.rendered.get(state.selected) else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let payload = match &cand.raw.data {\n            lattice_completion::CandidateData::Extension { payload, .. } => payload,\n            _ => return,\n        };\n        if payload.len() != 4 {\n            return;\n        }\n        let active_idx = u32::from_le_bytes([\n            payload[0],\n            payload[1],\n            payload[2],\n            payload[3],\n        ]) as usize;\n        if active_idx != outcome.meta_index {\n            return;\n        }\n        // Build the body from the freshly-resolved meta.\n        // (Direct field access here, since `docs_body_for_selected`\n        // would re-borrow self.)\n        let detail = self\n            .insert_completion_lsp_meta\n            .get(outcome.meta_index)\n            .and_then(|m| m.detail.clone())\n            .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n            .map(|s| format!(\"```\\n{s}\\n```\"));\n        let docs = self\n            .insert_completion_lsp_meta\n            .get(outcome.meta_index)\n            .and_then(|m| m.documentation.clone())\n            .filter(|s| !s.is_empty());\n        doc_popup.body = match (detail, docs) {\n            (Some(d), Some(b)) => Some(format!(\"{d}\\n\\n{b}\")),\n            (Some(d), None) => Some(d),\n            (None, Some(b)) => Some(b),\n            (None, None) => Some(\"(no documentation)\".to_string()),\n        };\n        doc_popup.scroll = 0;\n    }\n\n    /// Per-frame drain hook -- merge any LSP completion\n    /// response into the active popup's `raw` set, refilter,\n    /// and update the `lsp_incomplete` flag.\n    pub fn drain_pending_insert_completion_lsp(&mut self) {\n        let Some(mut rx) = self.pending_insert_completion_lsp_rx.take() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let mut latest: Option<InsertCompletionLspOutcome> = None;\n        while let Ok(o) = rx.try_recv() {\n            latest = Some(o);\n        }\n        self.pending_insert_completion_lsp_rx = Some(rx);\n        let outcome = match latest {\n            Some(o) => o,\n            None => return,\n        };\n        self.pending_insert_completion_lsp_token = None;\n        let Some(state) = self.insert_completion.as_mut() else {\n            // Popup closed before the response arrived; drop it.\n            self.insert_completion_lsp_meta.clear();\n            return;\n        };\n        match outcome {\n            InsertCompletionLspOutcome::NoServers => {\n                // Nothing to merge; sync sources stand alone.\n            }\n            InsertCompletionLspOutcome::Items {\n                items,\n                is_incomplete,\n            } => {\n                // Drop any prior LSP rows from raw + meta.\n                state.raw.retain(|c| {\n                    !matches!(\n                        c.data,\n                        lattice_completion::CandidateData::Extension {\n                            kind_id: LSP_COMPLETION_KIND_ID,\n                            ..\n                        }\n                    )\n                });\n                self.insert_completion_lsp_meta.clear();\n                // Append fresh items. Index into the meta vec\n                // is encoded as the candidate's\n                // Extension.payload.\n                for (i, meta) in items.into_iter().enumerate() {\n                    let display = match meta.detail.as_ref() {\n                        Some(d) => format!(\"{}  {}\", meta.label, d),\n                        None => meta.label.clone(),\n                    };\n                    let match_text = meta\n                        .filter_text\n                        .clone()\n                        .unwrap_or_else(|| meta.label.clone());\n                    let payload = (i as u32).to_le_bytes().to_vec();\n                    let mut raw = lattice_completion::RawCandidate::plain(\n                        match_text,\n                        lattice_completion::CandidateKind::Plain,\n                    )\n                    .with_source(lattice_completion::SourceId::new(\n                        lattice_completion::LSP_COMPLETION_SOURCE_ID,\n                    ));\n                    raw.display = display;\n                    raw.data = lattice_completion::CandidateData::Extension {\n                        kind_id: LSP_COMPLETION_KIND_ID,\n                        payload,\n                    };\n                    state.raw.push(raw);\n                    self.insert_completion_lsp_meta.push(meta);\n                }\n                state.lsp_incomplete = is_incomplete;\n            }\n        }\n        // Refilter against the (now-merged) raw set.\n        // Inline mirror of `refilter_insert_completion`'s body\n        // (we have a mutable borrow on `state` here so calling\n        // the helper would re-borrow).\n        let matcher = lattice_completion::FuzzyInsertMatcher::new();\n        let mut scored: Vec<lattice_completion::ScoredCandidate> = state\n            .raw\n            .iter()\n            .filter_map(|raw| {\n                lattice_completion::CandidateMatcher::matches(\n                    &matcher,\n                    &state.query,\n                    raw,\n                )\n                .map(|(score, ranges)| lattice_completion::ScoredCandidate {\n                    raw: raw.clone(),\n                    score,\n                    match_ranges: ranges,\n                })\n            })\n            .collect();\n        let ranker = lattice_completion::InsertRanker::new();\n        // Same disjoint-borrow shape as the post-edit refresh\n        // path; combines per-source priority with the capped\n        // frequency lift.\n        let freq = &self.completion_accept_freq;\n        let config = &self.config;\n        let opts = &self.core_options;\n        ranker.rank_with_bonus(&mut scored, |raw| {\n            let priority = match raw.source.as_ref().map(|s| s.as_str()) {\n                Some(\"gen:lsp-completion\") => {\n                    *config.get(opts.completion_source_lsp_priority)\n                }\n                Some(\"gen:snippet\") => {\n                    *config.get(opts.completion_source_snippet_priority)\n                }\n                Some(\"gen:buffer-words\") => {\n                    *config.get(opts.completion_source_buffer_words_priority)\n                }\n                _ => 0,\n            }\n            .clamp(0, u32::MAX as i64) as u32;\n            let freq_bonus = freq\n                .get(&(raw.text.clone(), raw.kind))\n                .copied()\n                .unwrap_or(0)\n                .min(lattice_completion::InsertRanker::FREQUENCY_BONUS_CAP);\n            priority.saturating_add(freq_bonus)\n        });\n        state.rendered = scored\n            .into_iter()\n            .map(lattice_completion::RenderedCandidate::from_scored)\n            .collect();\n        dedup_rendered_by_text(&mut state.rendered);\n        if !state.rendered.is_empty() && state.selected >= state.rendered.len() {\n            state.selected = state.rendered.len() - 1;\n        }\n        if state.rendered.is_empty() {\n            // No matches after merge -- close the popup.\n            self.insert_completion = None;\n            self.insert_completion_lsp_meta.clear();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Look up the LSP metadata for a candidate via its\n    /// `CandidateData::Extension` payload. Returns `None` for\n    /// non-LSP candidates (buffer-words / future sync sources)\n    /// or when the index is out of range (shouldn't happen but\n    /// guard anyway).\n    pub(crate) fn lsp_completion_meta_for(\n        &self,\n        candidate: &lattice_completion::RenderedCandidate,\n    ) -> Option<&LspCompletionMeta> {\n        let lattice_completion::CandidateData::Extension {\n            kind_id,\n            payload,\n        } = &candidate.raw.data\n        else {\n            return None;\n        };\n        if *kind_id != LSP_COMPLETION_KIND_ID {\n            return None;\n        }\n        if payload.len() != 4 {\n            return None;\n        }\n        let idx = u32::from_le_bytes([\n            payload[0],\n            payload[1],\n            payload[2],\n            payload[3],\n        ]) as usize;\n        self.insert_completion_lsp_meta.get(idx)\n    }\n\n    // ---- end Insert-mode completion ----\n\n    /// `:complete` (Phase 4.2.g). Fires\n    /// `textDocument/completion` at the cursor; the merged\n    /// item list opens as a vertico picker. Multi-server\n    /// union; dedup by `(label, kind)`. Token rides on\n    /// follow-up requests.\n    fn do_lsp_completion_request(&mut self) {\n        if let Some(token) = self.pending_completion_token.take() {\n            token.cancel();\n        }\n        // Browse-style; not a tag-intent drill-down.\n        self.pending_tag_origin = None;\n        let Some(uri) = self\n            .buffer_uris\n            .get(&self.document_buffer_id)\n            .cloned()\n        else {\n            self.set_message(\n                EchoLevel::Info,\n                \"no LSP server attached to current buffer\".to_string(),\n            );\n            return;\n        };\n        let snapshot = self.document.snapshot();\n        let lsp_position = match app_to_lsp_position(&snapshot.buffer, self.cursor) {\n            Some(p) => p,\n            None => return,\n        };\n        // Compute the prefix replace range: walk back from the\n        // cursor over word characters. The server may override\n        // via `text_edit` per-item, but the heuristic is the\n        // fallback when items don't carry one.\n        let line_text = snapshot.buffer.line(self.cursor.line).unwrap_or_default();\n        let cursor_byte = self.cursor.byte as usize;\n        let mut start = cursor_byte;\n        let bytes = line_text.as_bytes();\n        while start > 0 && start <= bytes.len() && is_word_char_byte(bytes[start - 1]) {\n            start -= 1;\n        }\n        let prefix_start = start as u32;\n        let cursor_line = self.cursor.line;\n        let cursor_col = self.cursor.byte;\n        let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<CompletionOutcome>();\n        let token = lattice_protocol::CancellationToken::new();\n        self.pending_completion_rx = Some(rx);\n        self.pending_completion_token = Some(token.clone());\n        let lsp = self.lsp.clone();\n        crate::runtime::spawn_on_lsp_runtime(async move {\n            let handles: Vec<lattice_lsp::ServerHandle> =\n                { lsp.lock().await.servers_for(&uri) };\n            if handles.is_empty() {\n                let _ = tx.send(CompletionOutcome::NoServers);\n                return;\n            }\n            let mut all: Vec<CompletionItemRow> = Vec::new();\n            for handle in handles {\n                if token.is_cancelled() {\n                    return;\n                }\n                if !handle.capabilities().supports_completion() {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                let params = lsp_types::CompletionParams {\n                    text_document_position: lsp_types::TextDocumentPositionParams {\n                        text_document: lsp_types::TextDocumentIdentifier { uri: uri.clone() },\n                        position: lsp_position,\n                    },\n                    work_done_progress_params: Default::default(),\n                    partial_result_params: Default::default(),\n                    context: None,\n                };\n                if let Ok(Some(resp)) = handle.completion(params, token.clone()).await {\n                    let items = match resp {\n                        lsp_types::CompletionResponse::Array(items) => items,\n                        lsp_types::CompletionResponse::List(list) => list.items,\n                    };\n                    for ci in items {\n                        let label = ci.label;\n                        let kind_glyph = completion_kind_glyph(ci.kind);\n                        let detail = ci.detail.clone();\n                        let insert_text = ci\n                            .insert_text\n                            .clone()\n                            .unwrap_or_else(|| label.clone());\n                        all.push(CompletionItemRow {\n                            label,\n                            kind_glyph,\n                            detail,\n                            insert_text,\n                            replace_range: (prefix_start, cursor_col),\n                            line: cursor_line,\n                        });\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            // Dedup by (label, kind glyph) -- avoid two servers\n            // emitting the same name twice.\n            all.sort_by(|a, b| a.label.cmp(&b.label).then_with(|| a.kind_glyph.cmp(b.kind_glyph)));\n            all.dedup_by(|a, b| a.label == b.label && a.kind_glyph == b.kind_glyph);\n            let _ = tx.send(CompletionOutcome::Items(all));\n        });\n    }\n\n    /// Drain queued LSP completion responses and open a picker.\n    /// `NoServers` echoes; empty list echoes.\n    pub fn drain_pending_completion(&mut self) {\n        let Some(mut rx) = self.pending_completion_rx.take() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let mut latest: Option<CompletionOutcome> = None;\n        while let Ok(o) = rx.try_recv() {\n            latest = Some(o);\n        }\n        self.pending_completion_rx = Some(rx);\n        let outcome = match latest {\n            Some(o) => o,\n            None => return,\n        };\n        self.pending_completion_token = None;\n        match outcome {\n            CompletionOutcome::NoServers => {\n                self.set_message(\n                    EchoLevel::Info,\n                    \"no LSP server attached\".to_string(),\n                );\n            }\n            CompletionOutcome::Items(items) => {\n                if items.is_empty() {\n                    self.set_message(EchoLevel::Info, \"no completions\".to_string());\n                    return;\n                }\n                let total = items.len();\n                let pairs: Vec<(\n                    lattice_completion::RawCandidate,\n                    crate::picker::RoutingPayload,\n                )> = items\n                    .iter()\n                    .enumerate()\n                    .map(|(i, item)| {\n                        let mut c = lattice_completion::RawCandidate::plain(\n                            item.label.clone(),\n                            lattice_completion::CandidateKind::Plain,\n                        );\n                        c.display = match &item.detail {\n                            Some(d) => format!(\"{} {}  {d}\", item.kind_glyph, item.label),\n                            None => format!(\"{} {}\", item.kind_glyph, item.label),\n                        };\n                        (\n                            c,\n                            crate::picker::RoutingPayload::LspCompletion {\n                                index: i as u32,\n                            },\n                        )\n                    })\n                    .collect();\n                self.pending_completion_items = Some(items);\n                let mut p = crate::picker::Picker::new(\n                    format!(\"complete ({total})\"),\n                    crate::picker::PickerSource::LspLocations,\n                    crate::picker::PickerAction::AcceptLspCompletion,\n                );\n                p.set_raw_candidates_with_routing(pairs);\n                self.picker = Some(p);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// `:lsp-signature-help` (Phase 4.3). Fan-out across\n    /// attached servers; first non-empty `SignatureHelp`\n    /// response wins (per docs/lsp-architecture.md §7b\n    /// \"First non-empty wins. Signatures are usually language-\n    /// specific; merging rarely useful.\").\n    fn do_lsp_signature_help_request(&mut self) {\n        if let Some(token) = self.pending_signature_help_token.take() {\n            token.cancel();\n        }\n        let Some(uri) = self\n            .buffer_uris\n            .get(&self.document_buffer_id)\n            .cloned()\n        else {\n            self.set_message(\n                EchoLevel::Info,\n                \"no LSP server attached to current buffer\".to_string(),\n            );\n            return;\n        };\n        let snapshot = self.document.snapshot();\n        let lsp_position = match app_to_lsp_position(&snapshot.buffer, self.cursor) {\n            Some(p) => p,\n            None => return,\n        };\n        let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<SignatureHelpOutcome>();\n        let token = lattice_protocol::CancellationToken::new();\n        self.pending_signature_help_rx = Some(rx);\n        self.pending_signature_help_token = Some(token.clone());\n        let lsp = self.lsp.clone();\n        crate::runtime::spawn_on_lsp_runtime(async move {\n            let handles: Vec<lattice_lsp::ServerHandle> =\n                { lsp.lock().await.servers_for(&uri) };\n            if handles.is_empty() {\n                let _ = tx.send(SignatureHelpOutcome::NoServers);\n                return;\n            }\n            for handle in handles {\n                if token.is_cancelled() {\n                    return;\n                }\n                if !handle.capabilities().supports_signature_help() {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                let params = lsp_types::SignatureHelpParams {\n                    text_document_position_params: lsp_types::TextDocumentPositionParams {\n                        text_document: lsp_types::TextDocumentIdentifier { uri: uri.clone() },\n                        position: lsp_position,\n                    },\n                    work_done_progress_params: Default::default(),\n                    context: None,\n                };\n                if let Ok(Some(sh)) = handle.signature_help(params, token.clone()).await {\n                    let body = signature_help_to_markdown(&sh);\n                    if !body.is_empty() {\n                        let _ = tx.send(SignatureHelpOutcome::Body(body));\n                        return;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            let _ = tx.send(SignatureHelpOutcome::Body(String::new()));\n        });\n    }\n\n    /// Drain queued signature-help responses. A non-empty body\n    /// renders into the popup; empty echoes \"no signature info\";\n    /// `NoServers` echoes the standard \"no LSP server\" message.\n    pub fn drain_pending_signature_help(&mut self) {\n        let Some(mut rx) = self.pending_signature_help_rx.take() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let mut latest: Option<SignatureHelpOutcome> = None;\n        while let Ok(o) = rx.try_recv() {\n            latest = Some(o);\n        }\n        self.pending_signature_help_rx = Some(rx);\n        let outcome = match latest {\n            Some(o) => o,\n            None => return,\n        };\n        self.pending_signature_help_token = None;\n        match outcome {\n            SignatureHelpOutcome::NoServers => {\n                self.set_message(\n                    EchoLevel::Info,\n                    \"no LSP server attached to current buffer\".to_string(),\n                );\n            }\n            SignatureHelpOutcome::Body(body) if body.is_empty() => {\n                self.set_message(EchoLevel::Info, \"no signature info\".to_string());\n            }\n            SignatureHelpOutcome::Body(body) => {\n                self.do_open_hover(&body);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// `<C-t>` -- pop the tag stack (vim's `:pop`). LIFO walk\n    /// back through the chain of `gd` / `gD` / `gy` / `gI`\n    /// drill-downs. Echoes \"tag stack empty\" with no entries.\n    /// Restores the recorded buffer (when cross-file) and\n    /// position; pushes the post-pop cursor onto the unified\n    /// position-history ring (PluginPush) so `<C-o>` continues\n    /// to walk the chronological jump record after a `<C-t>`\n    /// step.\n    fn do_tag_stack_pop(&mut self) {\n        let Some(entry) = self.tag_stack.pop() else {\n            self.set_message(EchoLevel::Info, \"tag stack empty\".to_string());\n            return;\n        };\n        // Push the *current* cursor onto the jump list before\n        // walking back so `<C-i>` returns to the post-pop spot.\n        self.push_position_history(self.cursor, PositionSource::PluginPush);\n        // If the recorded buffer differs from the active one,\n        // switch to it. The match is structural: prefer the\n        // exact `buffer_id` if it still exists in the registry,\n        // else any buffer of the recorded `buffer` kind.\n        let active_id = self.active_pane_buffer_id();\n        if entry.buffer_id != active_id {\n            // Best-effort activate; if the original buffer is\n            // gone (closed) the cursor still moves on whatever\n            // buffer is active. Acceptable v1 behaviour --\n            // future passes can echo \"tag origin buffer gone\"\n            // or hop to the alternate of the same kind.\n            if self.buffers.get(entry.buffer_id).is_some() {\n                self.activate_buffer(entry.buffer_id);\n            }\n        }\n        // Clamp to current buffer extents in case the doc was\n        // edited after the tag-stack push.\n        let buffer = self.active_text();\n        let last = last_addressable_line(&buffer);\n        let line = entry.position.line.min(last);\n        let len = line_byte_len(&buffer, line);\n        let col = entry.position.byte.min(len);\n        self.cursor = Position::new(line, col);\n        let label = if entry.label.is_empty() {\n            format!(\"tag pop -> ({},{})\", line + 1, col + 1)\n        } else {\n            format!(\"tag pop -> {} ({},{})\", entry.label, line + 1, col + 1)\n        };\n        self.set_message(EchoLevel::Info, label);\n    }\n\n    /// Jump to `path:line:col` (LSP 0-based line, utf-8 byte\n    /// column). Single entrypoint shared by the picker accept\n    /// path (`JumpToLspLocation`) and the `do_help_follow_link`\n    /// Source-link dispatch. Pushes the pre-jump cursor onto\n    /// position history with `PluginPush` so `<C-o>` walks back.\n    fn jump_to_file_line_col(&mut self, path: &std::path::Path, line: u32, col: u32) {\n        // Push pre-jump cursor before any state mutates.\n        self.push_position_history(self.cursor, PositionSource::PluginPush);\n\n        let same_buffer = self\n            .document\n            .path()\n            .map(|p| p == path)\n            .unwrap_or(false);\n        if !same_buffer {\n            self.do_edit(Some(path.to_path_buf()), false);\n        }\n        // Clamp the target line to the buffer's line count so a\n        // stale picker entry doesn't crash with an out-of-range\n        // cursor (e.g. user edited the file after the picker\n        // populated). `last_addressable_line` accounts for\n        // ropey's trailing-newline pseudo-line.\n        let snap = self.document.snapshot();\n        let line = line.min(last_addressable_line(&snap.buffer));\n        let line_len = line_byte_len(&snap.buffer, line);\n        let col = col.min(line_len);\n        self.cursor = Position::new(line, col);\n    }\n\n    /// Open `*lsp:<server_id>*` in the active pane via the\n    /// in-pane help registry path. Used by both the picker\n    /// accept dispatcher and the direct ex-command short path\n    /// when only one instance matches.\n    fn open_lsp_log_in_pane(&mut self, server_id: &str) {\n        let arc: std::sync::Arc<str> = std::sync::Arc::from(server_id);\n        let buffer = crate::help::HelpBuffer::lsp_server_log(&self.lsp_logger, &arc)\n            .with_markdown_syntax(self.lang_registry.clone());\n        self.open_help_in_pane(buffer);\n    }\n\n    /// Open `*lsp:<server_id>:trace*` in the active pane. Pure\n    /// view -- the trace toggle is `:lsp-trace <server>` and is\n    /// independent of opening / closing this buffer.\n    fn open_lsp_trace_log_in_pane(&mut self, server_id: &str) {\n        let arc: std::sync::Arc<str> = std::sync::Arc::from(server_id);\n        let buffer = crate::help::HelpBuffer::lsp_server_trace(&self.lsp_logger, &arc)\n            .with_markdown_syntax(self.lang_registry.clone());\n        self.open_help_in_pane(buffer);\n    }\n\n    /// Drain queued [`lattice_protocol::Event::LspLogPushed`]\n    /// events (Phase 4) and refresh any open log / trace\n    /// help buffers from the logger snapshot. Called once per\n    /// main-loop tick + at the end of any path that pushes a log\n    /// record synchronously.\n    ///\n    /// Cheap when no log buffers are open: the refresh path\n    /// short-circuits on `BufferRegistry::help_with_title`\n    /// missing the title. When buffers ARE open the rebuild walks\n    /// the logger ring (≤ 10k records) and replaces the rope --\n    /// well within frame budget for the editor's scale.\n    pub fn drain_lsp_log_events(&mut self) {\n        let Some(mut rx) = self.lsp_log_event_rx.take() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        // Coalesce: collect every drained event's scope, then\n        // refresh each unique scope at most once. A burst of\n        // 100 trace records on one server -> one refresh.\n        let mut subsystem = false;\n        let mut server_logs: std::collections::HashSet<String> =\n            std::collections::HashSet::new();\n        let mut server_traces: std::collections::HashSet<String> =\n            std::collections::HashSet::new();\n        while let Ok(event) = rx.try_recv() {\n            if let lattice_protocol::Event::LspLogPushed {\n                server_id,\n                level,\n                source,\n                ..\n            } = event\n            {\n                match server_id {\n                    None => subsystem = true,\n                    Some(id) => {\n                        // Trace records (level == \"trace\" OR\n                        // source == \"trace\") refresh the trace\n                        // view; everything else refreshes the\n                        // per-server log view (which excludes\n                        // trace records by filter).\n                        if level == \"trace\" || source == \"trace\" {\n                            server_traces.insert(id);\n                        } else {\n                            server_logs.insert(id);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if subsystem {\n            self.refresh_lsp_log_buffer_subsystem();\n        }\n        for id in server_logs {\n            self.refresh_lsp_log_buffer_per_server(&id);\n        }\n        for id in server_traces {\n            self.refresh_lsp_trace_buffer(&id);\n        }\n        self.lsp_log_event_rx = Some(rx);\n    }\n\n    /// Rebuild the `*lsp*` (subsystem-wide) help buffer from the\n    /// logger snapshot, preserving cursor + scroll. No-op when\n    /// the buffer isn't currently open.\n    fn refresh_lsp_log_buffer_subsystem(&mut self) {\n        let Some(id) = self.buffers.help_with_title(\"lsp\") else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let new_buf = crate::help::HelpBuffer::lsp_global_log(&self.lsp_logger)\n            .with_markdown_syntax(self.lang_registry.clone());\n        self.replace_help_buffer_preserving_cursor(id, new_buf);\n    }\n\n    /// Rebuild `*lsp:<server_id>*` from the logger snapshot.\n    fn refresh_lsp_log_buffer_per_server(&mut self, server_id: &str) {\n        let title = format!(\"lsp:{server_id}\");\n        let Some(id) = self.buffers.help_with_title(&title) else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let arc: std::sync::Arc<str> = std::sync::Arc::from(server_id);\n        let new_buf = crate::help::HelpBuffer::lsp_server_log(&self.lsp_logger, &arc)\n            .with_markdown_syntax(self.lang_registry.clone());\n        self.replace_help_buffer_preserving_cursor(id, new_buf);\n    }\n\n    /// Rebuild `*lsp:<server_id>:trace*` from the logger snapshot.\n    fn refresh_lsp_trace_buffer(&mut self, server_id: &str) {\n        let title = format!(\"lsp:{server_id}:trace\");\n        let Some(id) = self.buffers.help_with_title(&title) else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let arc: std::sync::Arc<str> = std::sync::Arc::from(server_id);\n        let new_buf = crate::help::HelpBuffer::lsp_server_trace(&self.lsp_logger, &arc)\n            .with_markdown_syntax(self.lang_registry.clone());\n        self.replace_help_buffer_preserving_cursor(id, new_buf);\n    }\n\n    /// Atomically replace a registry-tracked help buffer's body\n    /// with `new_buf`, preserving the existing buffer id +\n    /// cursor + scroll so the user's view stays put across the\n    /// rebuild. Clamps cursor to the new content's line bounds.\n    /// Also syncs `App.help_buffer` (the popup hot-path mirror)\n    /// when it points at the same id.\n    fn replace_help_buffer_preserving_cursor(\n        &mut self,\n        id: BufferId,\n        mut new_buf: crate::help::HelpBuffer,\n    ) {\n        let (cur, scr) = match self.buffers.help(id) {\n            Some(h) => (h.cursor, h.scroll),\n            None => return,\n        };\n        new_buf.id = id;\n        new_buf.cursor = cur;\n        new_buf.scroll = scr;\n        let line_count = new_buf.line_count() as u32;\n        if line_count > 0 && new_buf.cursor.line >= line_count {\n            new_buf.cursor.line = line_count - 1;\n        }\n        if let Some(slot) = self.buffers.help_mut(id) {\n            *slot = new_buf;\n        }\n        // Sync the popup hot-path mirror when active.\n        if self.help_buffer.as_ref().map(|h| h.id) == Some(id)\n            && let Some(reg) = self.buffers.help(id)\n        {\n            self.help_buffer = Some(reg.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// `:bd[elete]` -- close the active buffer (whichever the\n    /// active pane shows). v1 picks any other buffer to activate;\n    /// if no others remain, the close is rejected. For document\n    /// buffers `!` bypasses the dirty check; tree buffers are\n    /// always read-only and skip the dirty guard.\n    fn do_buffer_delete(&mut self, force: bool) {\n        if self.buffers.len() <= 1 {\n            self.set_message(\n                EchoLevel::Error,\n                \"Cannot delete the only buffer\".to_string(),\n            );\n            return;\n        }\n        let to_remove = self.active_pane_buffer_id();\n        // Dirty check applies to documents only.\n        if let Some(d) = self.buffers.document(to_remove)\n            && !force\n            && d.handle.dirty()\n        {\n            self.set_message(\n                EchoLevel::Error,\n                \"no write since last change (add ! to override)\".to_string(),\n            );\n            return;\n        }\n        // Pick a successor (any other buffer in id order).\n        let ids = self.buffers.sorted_ids();\n        let Some(successor) = ids.iter().copied().find(|id| *id != to_remove) else {\n            return;\n        };\n        self.activate_buffer(successor);\n        // Detach from LSP before dropping the buffer registry\n        // entry so the supervisor sees the URI go away while the\n        // BufferId is still mapped.\n        self.lsp_close_buffer(to_remove);\n        self.buffers.remove(to_remove);\n        // Re-point any pane still referencing the removed buffer.\n        let new_id = self.active_pane_buffer_id();\n        let new_kind = self.active_buffer;\n        for pane in self.pane_tree.leaves_mut() {\n            if pane.buffer_id == to_remove {\n                pane.buffer_id = new_id;\n                pane.buffer = new_kind;\n            }\n        }\n        self.set_message(EchoLevel::Info, format!(\"buffer #{} deleted\", to_remove.0));\n    }\n\n    /// Switch the active pane to whatever buffer `id` references,\n    /// regardless of kind. Document buffers route through\n    /// [`Self::activate_document`]; tree buffers update the active\n    /// pane + load the tree's stash; help buffers go through\n    /// [`Self::activate_help_in_pane`] (Phase 1 wiring).\n    pub fn activate_buffer(&mut self, id: BufferId) {\n        let kind = match self.buffers.get(id) {\n            Some(entry) => entry.kind(),\n            None => {\n                self.set_message(EchoLevel::Error, format!(\"buffer #{} not found\", id.0));\n                return;\n            }\n        };\n        match kind {\n            BufferKind::Document => self.activate_document(id),\n            BufferKind::FileTree => self.activate_file_tree(id),\n            BufferKind::Help => self.activate_help_in_pane(id),\n            BufferKind::Oil => self.activate_oil(id),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Switch the active pane to the file-tree buffer with `id`.\n    /// Snapshots the current active state first; the pane's\n    /// stashed cursor / scroll load into the tree's hot fields\n    /// via [`Self::load_active_pane`].\n    pub fn activate_file_tree(&mut self, id: BufferId) {\n        if self.buffers.file_tree(id).is_none() {\n            self.set_message(EchoLevel::Error, format!(\"buffer #{} not a tree\", id.0));\n            return;\n        }\n        if id == self.active_pane_buffer_id() && matches!(self.active_buffer, BufferKind::FileTree)\n        {\n            return;\n        }\n        self.snapshot_active_pane();\n        self.snapshot_active_document();\n        // Load the tree's stash into the App's hot-path cursor /\n        // scroll. After this, `self.cursor` / `self.scroll` are\n        // the tree's -- motion / scroll / search code reads /\n        // writes them uniformly, no per-kind branches needed.\n        let (stash_cursor, stash_scroll) = self\n            .buffers\n            .file_tree(id)\n            .map(|t| (t.cursor, t.scroll as u32))\n            .unwrap_or((Position::ZERO, 0));\n        self.cursor = stash_cursor;\n        self.scroll = stash_scroll;\n        self.active_buffer = BufferKind::FileTree;\n        let pane = self.pane_tree.active_mut();\n        pane.buffer = BufferKind::FileTree;\n        pane.buffer_id = id;\n        pane.cursor = stash_cursor;\n        pane.scroll = stash_scroll;\n    }\n\n    /// Switch the active pane to the oil buffer with `id`.\n    pub fn activate_oil(&mut self, id: BufferId) {\n        if self.buffers.oil(id).is_none() {\n            return;\n        }\n        let oil_cursor = self.buffers.oil(id).map(|o| o.cursor).unwrap_or(Position::ZERO);\n        let oil_scroll = self.buffers.oil(id).map(|o| o.scroll).unwrap_or(0);\n        self.active_buffer = BufferKind::Oil;\n        let pane = self.pane_tree.active_mut();\n        pane.buffer = BufferKind::Oil;\n        pane.buffer_id = id;\n        pane.cursor = oil_cursor;\n        pane.scroll = oil_scroll as u32;\n        self.cursor = oil_cursor;\n        self.scroll = oil_scroll as u32;\n        self.pending = Pending::None;\n    }\n\n    /// `:set [option | option=value | nooption | option?]`.\n    /// Parses against the shared typed-options\n    /// [`lattice_config::ConfigRegistry`] (DESIGN.md §5.12).\n    /// Boolean toggle / negate forms (`:set nu` / `:set nonu`),\n    /// query (`:set foo?`), and typed assignment (`:set tabstop=4`)\n    /// all flow through one path. Post-set side effects\n    /// (`relativenumber` ⇒ `number`, `foldmethod` ⇒ recompute folds,\n    /// every `ui.*` change ⇒ refresh derived theme styles) are\n    /// applied after a successful set in [`Self::apply_post_set`].\n    /// Load `~/.config/lattice/lattice.toml` (user) and\n    /// `<workspace_root>/.lattice/config.toml` (project) in\n    /// precedence order, applying scalar overrides to\n    /// `self.config` and bucketing structural sub-tables (per-\n    /// language overrides, plugin sections) into\n    /// `self.pending_config_structural_sections` for their\n    /// owners to drain.\n    ///\n    /// Called once by the runtime startup before the main loop\n    /// (so the first frame already reflects user overrides).\n    /// NOT called from `App::new` -- tests stay isolated from\n    /// the user's real `~/.config/lattice/`. Test fixtures that\n    /// want to exercise the load path can call this directly\n    /// with a synthesized workspace root.\n    ///\n    /// Loader diagnostics (parse errors, unknown keys,\n    /// validation rejects) collapse into a single echo at the\n    /// most-severe level: `Error` if any file failed to\n    /// parse / read, `Warn` if any key was rejected, otherwise\n    /// silent. Per-file `path:body` detail rides the message\n    /// body so the user can see *which* file complained.\n    pub fn load_persistent_config(&mut self, workspace_root: Option<&std::path::Path>) {\n        // The structural prefixes the App / future plugin host\n        // own. The per-language layer drains\n        // `completion.per-language.*`; the plugin host (Phase 7)\n        // will drain `plugin.*`. Both buckets accumulate here\n        // without further interpretation in this slice.\n        let prefixes = [\"completion.per-language\", \"plugin\"];\n        let outcome = lattice_config::load_default_paths(\n            &self.config,\n            workspace_root,\n            &prefixes,\n        );\n        // Re-derive theme + hot-path option cache after the\n        // loader's writes. ui.* and the cached options may have\n        // changed; missing this would leave the first frame\n        // rendering with stale derived state.\n        self.sync_theme_from_config();\n        self.rebuild_option_cache();\n        // Stash structural sections for the layers that own\n        // them. Subsequent slices drain via\n        // `take_pending_structural_section(prefix)`.\n        for (k, v) in outcome.structural {\n            self.pending_config_structural_sections.insert(k, v);\n        }\n        // Surface a single echo summarising loader diagnostics.\n        // The renderer's modeline only shows the latest echo,\n        // so multi-warn configs collapse into \"<count> issues\n        // (first: <body>)\". Severity is the max across the run.\n        if outcome.messages.is_empty() {\n            return;\n        }\n        let max_level = outcome\n            .messages\n            .iter()\n            .map(|m| m.level)\n            .max_by_key(|l| match l {\n                lattice_config::LoadMessageLevel::Error => 1,\n                lattice_config::LoadMessageLevel::Warning => 0,\n            })\n            .unwrap_or(lattice_config::LoadMessageLevel::Warning);\n        let echo_level = match max_level {\n            lattice_config::LoadMessageLevel::Error => EchoLevel::Error,\n            lattice_config::LoadMessageLevel::Warning => EchoLevel::Warn,\n        };\n        let count = outcome.messages.len();\n        let first = &outcome.messages[0];\n        let body = if count == 1 {\n            format!(\n                \"config: {}: {}\",\n                first.source.display(),\n                first.body,\n            )\n        } else {\n            format!(\n                \"config: {count} issues (first: {}: {})\",\n                first.source.display(),\n                first.body,\n            )\n        };\n        self.set_message(echo_level, body);\n    }\n\n    /// Drain every `completion.per-language.<lang>` structural\n    /// section the loader bucketed and merge each into\n    /// `self.per_language_completion`. Per-key TOML wins over\n    /// the spec defaults seeded at `App::new`; unset keys leave\n    /// the default in place.\n    ///\n    /// Called by the runtime startup right after\n    /// `load_persistent_config` finishes. Idempotent (the bucket\n    /// empties as we drain). Per-key parse warnings collapse\n    /// into a single echo at `Warn` level the same way the\n    /// loader's other diagnostics do.\n    pub fn apply_per_language_toml_overrides(&mut self) {\n        let paths = self.pending_structural_section_paths(\"completion.per-language\");\n        let mut warnings: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n        for path in paths {\n            let lang = match path.strip_prefix(\"completion.per-language.\") {\n                Some(s) if !s.is_empty() => s.to_string(),\n                _ => continue,\n            };\n            let Some(table) = self.take_pending_structural_section(&path) else {\n                continue;\n            };\n            let parsed = parse_per_language_overrides_table(&path, &table, &mut warnings);\n            self.per_language_completion\n                .entry(lang)\n                .or_default()\n                .merge(parsed);\n        }\n        if !warnings.is_empty() {\n            let count = warnings.len();\n            let body = if count == 1 {\n                format!(\"config: {}\", warnings[0])\n            } else {\n                format!(\"config: {count} per-language warnings (first: {})\", warnings[0])\n            };\n            self.set_message(EchoLevel::Warn, body);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Effective completion config for `language` -- per-language\n    /// override lays over the global typed option which lays\n    /// over the spec fallback. Used at every producer-side\n    /// enforcement seam (sync source filter, LSP fan-out, the\n    /// `auto_insert_single` check at popup-open).\n    pub fn effective_completion_for(\n        &self,\n        language: &str,\n    ) -> EffectiveCompletionConfig {\n        let overrides = self.per_language_completion.get(language);\n        EffectiveCompletionConfig {\n            sources: overrides.and_then(|o| o.sources.clone()),\n            // No global `completion.auto_trigger` typed option\n            // yet (auto-trigger firing itself lands in a future\n            // slice); fall back to false per spec.\n            auto_trigger: overrides.and_then(|o| o.auto_trigger).unwrap_or(false),\n            auto_insert_single: overrides\n                .and_then(|o| o.auto_insert_single)\n                .unwrap_or_else(|| self.completion_auto_insert_single()),\n            suppress_in: overrides\n                .and_then(|o| o.suppress_in.clone())\n                .unwrap_or_default(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Drain the structural section at `prefix` (an entry\n    /// matching one of the loader's structural prefixes,\n    /// e.g. `\"completion.per-language.markdown\"`). Removes it\n    /// from `pending_config_structural_sections`; subsequent\n    /// drains return `None`. Used by the per-language /\n    /// plugin-host layers to consume their TOML config without\n    /// leaving stale entries behind.\n    pub fn take_pending_structural_section(\n        &mut self,\n        full_path: &str,\n    ) -> Option<toml::Table> {\n        self.pending_config_structural_sections.remove(full_path)\n    }\n\n    /// Iterate the dotted paths of every pending structural\n    /// section whose path starts with `namespace.` (e.g.\n    /// `\"completion.per-language\"` returns the language ids).\n    /// Returned as owned `String`s to keep the borrow short --\n    /// callers typically follow up with\n    /// `take_pending_structural_section(full)` mutating the map.\n    pub fn pending_structural_section_paths(\n        &self,\n        namespace: &str,\n    ) -> Vec<String> {\n        let prefix = format!(\"{namespace}.\");\n        self.pending_config_structural_sections\n            .keys()\n            .filter(|k| k.starts_with(&prefix))\n            .cloned()\n            .collect()\n    }\n\n    fn do_set(&mut self, option: &str) {\n        let echo = match self.config.parse_and_set_command(option) {\n            Ok(echo) => echo,\n            Err(err) => {\n                self.set_message(EchoLevel::Error, err.to_string());\n                return;\n            }\n        };\n        // Drain any cascade events the set just enqueued so the\n        // user sees the side effects (recompute folds, theme\n        // refresh, ...) before the next frame draws. The runtime's\n        // main_loop also drains once per iteration as a backstop\n        // for writes that originate outside the keystroke path\n        // (plugin tasks, future LSP-driven config writes).\n        self.drain_option_changes();\n        self.set_message(EchoLevel::Info, echo);\n    }\n\n    /// Drain queued [`Event::OptionChanged`] events from the App's\n    /// own bus subscription and apply per-option cascades on the\n    /// App's main thread.\n    ///\n    /// Why a channel and not a callback: typed-option writes can\n    /// originate from anywhere -- the cmdline, plugin tasks\n    /// (Phase 7), the customize buffer view (post-1.0), or future\n    /// LSP-driven config writes. The publisher closure on the\n    /// registry runs *on the calling thread*, which may not be\n    /// the App's. Routing every cascade through this channel\n    /// gives us:\n    ///\n    /// - **No re-entrancy on the registry mutex**: the cascade\n    ///   runs after the publish path drops every lock. A cascade\n    ///   that itself calls `config.set` (e.g. `relativenumber=true`\n    ///   ⇒ `number=true`) just queues another event -- the\n    ///   `while let Ok` loop picks it up on the next iteration.\n    /// - **No render-thread blocking**: drains happen at known\n    ///   points (top of main_loop iteration, end of `do_set`).\n    ///   Plugins doing heavy work in their own subscriptions\n    ///   never delay a keystroke.\n    /// - **One source of truth for the cascade logic**: any\n    ///   typed-option write goes through this hook regardless of\n    ///   how the write was triggered. Pre-bus the cascade lived\n    ///   on the cmdline path only and direct `config.set` calls\n    ///   silently skipped it.\n    ///\n    /// `Manual` foldmethod, no-op cascades, and unmatched options\n    /// all return early so the drain is cheap when nothing\n    /// substantive needs to happen.\n    pub fn drain_option_changes(&mut self) {\n        // Take the receiver to dodge the borrow checker (we want\n        // to mutate `self` for cascades while reading from the rx).\n        // Always restored after the loop; the `Option` is purely a\n        // borrow gymnastic, never observed in any other state.\n        let mut rx = match self.option_change_rx.take() {\n            Some(rx) => rx,\n            None => return,\n        };\n        while let Ok(event) = rx.try_recv() {\n            if let Event::OptionChanged { name, .. } = event {\n                self.apply_option_cascade(&name);\n            }\n        }\n        self.option_change_rx = Some(rx);\n    }\n\n    /// Run the per-option cascade for `canonical_name` (already\n    /// resolved by `Event::OptionChanged.name`, which is always\n    /// the canonical name regardless of which alias the user\n    /// typed).\n    fn apply_option_cascade(&mut self, canonical_name: &str) {\n        // Refresh the hot-path cache so subsequent reads from\n        // `app.show_line_numbers()` etc. see the new value.\n        // Cheap (~300ns total for all 9 options); only runs when\n        // an option actually changed, never on every frame.\n        self.rebuild_option_cache();\n        match canonical_name {\n            \"relativenumber\" => {\n                // Vim cascade: `:set rnu` implies `:set nu` so the\n                // gutter renders at all. The reverse (`:set nornu`)\n                // does NOT clear `nu` -- preserves user intent.\n                // Conditional on the new value being `true`, which\n                // we re-read through the typed handle (cheap).\n                if self.relative_line_numbers() {\n                    let _ = self.config.set(self.core_options.number, true);\n                }\n            }\n            \"foldmethod\" => {\n                // Recompute folds against the new method. Idempotent\n                // and cheap when method is `Manual` (the recompute\n                // returns immediately).\n                self.recompute_folds();\n            }\n            n if n.starts_with(\"ui.\") => {\n                self.sync_theme_from_config();\n            }\n            _ => {}\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Re-derive `App.theme`'s renderer-specific [`Style`] values\n    /// from the current `ui.*` option values in the config. Called\n    /// at App-init time (after registration) and on every `:set\n    /// ui.*` so the cached theme stays in lockstep with the\n    /// canonical primitives in config.\n    pub fn sync_theme_from_config(&mut self) {\n        use ratatui::style::Style;\n        // ui.dim_inactive -- bool flag projected directly.\n        self.theme.dim_inactive_panes = *self.config.get(self.tui_options.dim_inactive);\n        // ui.separator -- one-character glyph for the vertical\n        // pane divider. Validated to len==1 at parse; fall back to\n        // the default if a forged value sneaks through.\n        let sep = self.config.get(self.tui_options.separator);\n        self.theme.pane_separator_vertical = sep.chars().next().unwrap_or('│');\n        // ui.separator_color -- color name; parse_color returned\n        // Ok during validate so unwrap-via-fallback is safe.\n        let sep_color = self.config.get(self.tui_options.separator_color);\n        if let Ok(c) = crate::theme::parse_color(&sep_color) {\n            self.theme.pane_separator = Style::default().fg(c);\n        }\n        // ui.statusline_active_fg -- foreground only; preserve any\n        // existing modifiers / background by chaining `.fg(c)` on\n        // the current style.\n        let active_fg = self.config.get(self.tui_options.statusline_active_fg);\n        if let Ok(c) = crate::theme::parse_color(&active_fg) {\n            self.theme.pane_status_active = self.theme.pane_status_active.fg(c);\n        }\n        let inactive_fg = self.config.get(self.tui_options.statusline_inactive_fg);\n        if let Ok(c) = crate::theme::parse_color(&inactive_fg) {\n            self.theme.pane_status_inactive = self.theme.pane_status_inactive.fg(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// `:describe-option <name>` (DESIGN.md §5.11). Renders the\n    /// option's metadata + current value into a help buffer.\n    fn do_describe_option(&mut self, name: &str) {\n        let Some(spec) = self.config.lookup(name) else {\n            self.set_message(EchoLevel::Error, format!(\"E518: Unknown option: {name}\"));\n            return;\n        };\n        let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n        lines.push(format!(\"# {}\", spec.name()));\n        if !spec.aliases().is_empty() {\n            lines.push(format!(\"aliases: {}\", spec.aliases().join(\", \")));\n        }\n        lines.push(format!(\"type:    {}\", spec.type_label()));\n        lines.push(format!(\"default: {}\", spec.default_formatted()));\n        lines.push(format!(\"current: {}\", spec.get_formatted()));\n        if let Some(values) = spec.enumerate_values() {\n            lines.push(format!(\"values:  {}\", values.join(\", \")));\n        }\n        lines.push(String::new());\n        lines.push(spec.doc().to_string());\n        self.open_help(\n            HelpBuffer::from_lines(format!(\"describe-option {name}\"), lines)\n                .with_markdown_syntax(self.lang_registry.clone()),\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// `K` (LSP hover) response handler / `:hover [markdown]`.\n    /// Enters **State A**: popup overlay shown, focus stays on\n    /// the main buffer. The popup auto-dismisses on the next\n    /// motion (apply()'s post-dispatch hook) since it's anchored\n    /// to the symbol the user K'd. To navigate inside the popup\n    /// the user presses `K` again, which `do_lsp_hover_request`\n    /// recognises as \"focus into popup\" -> State B.\n    fn do_open_hover(&mut self, markdown: &str) {\n        let lines: Vec<String> = markdown.split('\\n').map(String::from).collect();\n        let buffer = crate::help::HelpBuffer::from_lines(\"hover\", lines)\n            .with_markdown_syntax(self.lang_registry.clone());\n        // State A: just set the help_buffer. Active stays on the\n        // main buffer; self.cursor untouched. prev_pane_for_help\n        // remains `None` -- the State-A auto-dismiss key.\n        self.help_buffer = Some(buffer);\n    }\n\n    /// **State A -> State B**: focus moves into the popup. After\n    /// this, the popup behaves like any other buffer -- vim\n    /// grammar (motions, `/` search, `n`/`N`, `gg`/`G`, `:` ex\n    /// commands) operates on the popup's content; the doc behind\n    /// is frozen. Dismiss with `<Esc>` / `q` returns focus to\n    /// the doc at the cursor it was on.\n    ///\n    /// `prev_pane_for_help` is the dismiss-restore stash and\n    /// also signals \"we're in State B\" to the auto-dismiss-on-\n    /// motion hook -- when it's `Some`, motion doesn't close the\n    /// popup.\n    fn focus_help_popup(&mut self) {\n        let Some(help) = self.help_buffer.as_ref() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let stash_cursor = help.cursor;\n        let stash_scroll = help.scroll as u32;\n        // Capture pre-State-B state so dismiss restores cleanly.\n        let active = self.pane_tree.active();\n        self.prev_pane_for_help = Some(PrevPaneState {\n            buffer: active.buffer,\n            buffer_id: active.buffer_id,\n            cursor: self.cursor,\n            scroll: self.scroll,\n        });\n        // Sync active pane's cursor / scroll stash *before*\n        // swapping `active_buffer` to Help. The renderer reads\n        // `pane.cursor` for any pane whose buffer kind doesn't\n        // match `active_buffer`; without this sync the doc would\n        // appear to jump to wherever pane.cursor was last set\n        // (often (0,0) on a fresh app).\n        self.snapshot_active_pane();\n        self.cursor = stash_cursor;\n        self.scroll = stash_scroll;\n        self.active_buffer = BufferKind::Help;\n        self.pending = Pending::None;\n    }\n\n    /// `K` (Phase 4.2.b). Send `textDocument/hover` to every LSP\n    /// server attached to the active document; the spawned task\n    /// awaits the actor's response on the LSP runtime, so the\n    /// keystroke handler returns instantly. The markdown body\n    /// arrives back through [`Self::pending_hover_rx`] and the\n    /// next frame's [`Self::drain_pending_hover`] feeds it into\n    /// the popup.\n    ///\n    /// **Multi-server merge** is \"first non-empty wins\" for\n    /// 4.2.b. The `--- {server-name} ---` concat-with-separators\n    /// strategy spec'd in `docs/lsp-architecture.md` lands as a\n    /// follow-up; today the simpler shape is enough to validate\n    /// the end-to-end plumbing.\n    ///\n    /// **Cancellation**: any prior in-flight hover's token is\n    /// flipped before the new request fires, so a slow server\n    /// can't drop a stale popup over the new cursor position.\n    fn do_lsp_hover_request(&mut self) {\n        // Already focused into the popup (State B) -- K is a\n        // no-op. To get a fresh hover the user dismisses with\n        // Esc / q, repositions in the doc, then presses K.\n        if matches!(self.active_buffer, BufferKind::Help) {\n            return;\n        }\n        // Popup shown but focus still on main buffer (State A) --\n        // second K transfers focus into the popup. No new LSP\n        // request fires; we just promote.\n        if self.help_buffer.is_some() {\n            self.focus_help_popup();\n            return;\n        }\n        // First K -- fire a fresh hover request. Cancel any\n        // in-flight first.\n        if let Some(token) = self.pending_hover_token.take() {\n            token.cancel();\n        }\n\n        // Resolve the active buffer's URI. No URI = no LSP for\n        // this buffer (e.g. unsaved scratch); echo + bail.\n        let Some(uri) = self\n            .buffer_uris\n            .get(&self.document_buffer_id)\n            .cloned()\n        else {\n            self.set_message(\n                EchoLevel::Info,\n                \"no LSP server attached to current buffer\".to_string(),\n            );\n            return;\n        };\n\n        // Build the LSP-side cursor position. App's cursor is\n        // (line, col_byte) in utf-8; LSP wants utf-16 columns.\n        // The lattice-lsp::position::Encoding-aware conversion\n        // walks the line; for hover, exact column matters\n        // because servers gate the lookup on the symbol under\n        // the cursor.\n        let snapshot = self.document.snapshot();\n        let lsp_position = match app_to_lsp_position(&snapshot.buffer, self.cursor) {\n            Some(p) => p,\n            None => {\n                self.set_message(EchoLevel::Error, \"hover: cursor out of buffer\".to_string());\n                return;\n            }\n        };\n\n        // Fresh channel + token for this request.\n        let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<HoverOutcome>();\n        let token = lattice_protocol::CancellationToken::new();\n        self.pending_hover_rx = Some(rx);\n        self.pending_hover_token = Some(token.clone());\n\n        // Spawn the request on the LSP runtime so the keystroke\n        // handler returns instantly. The task walks every server\n        // attached to the buffer; first non-empty body wins. The\n        // task ALWAYS sends exactly one [`HoverOutcome`] (or\n        // nothing if cancelled mid-flight, in which case the\n        // drain just sees an idle channel) so the user always\n        // gets feedback.\n        //\n        // Trace each request into `:lsp-log` so users can\n        // correlate K presses with server responses -- helpful\n        // when rust-analyzer-style servers are still indexing\n        // on first launch and every hover returns null until the\n        // index settles (a real \"indexing in progress\" status\n        // segment lands as Phase 4.4 polish).\n        // Per-request traces flow into the per-server ring\n        // (`*lsp:<server>*`) -- not the global `*lsp*` ring --\n        // so `:lsp-server-log` lands the picker on the right\n        // place to inspect them. The logger's first arg gates\n        // routing: `None` = global ring; `Some(id)` = per-server\n        // ring keyed by id.\n        let lsp = self.lsp.clone();\n        let logger = self.lsp_logger.clone();\n        let request_started = std::time::Instant::now();\n        let request_uri = uri.as_str().to_string();\n        crate::runtime::spawn_on_lsp_runtime(async move {\n            // Snapshot the attached handles under the supervisor\n            // lock, then drop it before awaiting any per-server\n            // response (the lock is App-side; we don't hold it\n            // across awaits).\n            let handles: Vec<lattice_lsp::ServerHandle> =\n                { lsp.lock().await.servers_for(&uri) };\n            if handles.is_empty() {\n                let _ = tx.send(HoverOutcome::NoServers);\n                return;\n            }\n            let mut tried = 0usize;\n            for handle in handles {\n                if token.is_cancelled() {\n                    return;\n                }\n                tried += 1;\n                let params = lsp_types::HoverParams {\n                    text_document_position_params: lsp_types::TextDocumentPositionParams {\n                        text_document: lsp_types::TextDocumentIdentifier { uri: uri.clone() },\n                        position: lsp_position,\n                    },\n                    work_done_progress_params: Default::default(),\n                };\n                let server_id_arc: std::sync::Arc<str> =\n                    std::sync::Arc::from(handle.server_id());\n                // Trace the request landing per-server so the\n                // user can correlate K presses with responses\n                // from inside `:lsp-log <server>` /\n                // `:lsp-server-log` -> the right row's log.\n                logger.log(\n                    Some(&server_id_arc),\n                    lattice_lsp::LogLevel::Debug,\n                    lattice_lsp::LogSource::Client,\n                    format!(\n                        \"hover requested @ {request_uri} line {} character {}\",\n                        lsp_position.line, lsp_position.character\n                    ),\n                );\n                match handle.hover(params, token.clone()).await {\n                    Ok(Some(hover)) => {\n                        let body = hover_contents_to_markdown(&hover.contents);\n                        if !body.trim().is_empty() {\n                            logger.log(\n                                Some(&server_id_arc),\n                                lattice_lsp::LogLevel::Debug,\n                                lattice_lsp::LogSource::Client,\n                                format!(\n                                    \"hover reply: {} bytes after {:?}\",\n                                    body.len(),\n                                    request_started.elapsed()\n                                ),\n                            );\n                            let _ = tx.send(HoverOutcome::Body(body));\n                            return;\n                        }\n                        // Server replied but the body's empty.\n                        // rust-analyzer returns this while still\n                        // indexing -- highlight the pattern in\n                        // the per-server log.\n                        logger.log(\n                            Some(&server_id_arc),\n                            lattice_lsp::LogLevel::Debug,\n                            lattice_lsp::LogSource::Client,\n                            \"hover reply: empty body (server still indexing?)\".to_string(),\n                        );\n                    }\n                    Ok(None) => {\n                        logger.log(\n                            Some(&server_id_arc),\n                            lattice_lsp::LogLevel::Debug,\n                            lattice_lsp::LogSource::Client,\n                            \"hover reply: null (cursor not on a known symbol, or server still indexing)\"\n                                .to_string(),\n                        );\n                    }\n                    Err(e) => {\n                        // Cancelled / decode error / actor gone:\n                        // per-server Warn so the right log\n                        // surfaces the reason.\n                        logger.log(\n                            Some(&server_id_arc),\n                            lattice_lsp::LogLevel::Warn,\n                            lattice_lsp::LogSource::Client,\n                            format!(\"hover error: {e}\"),\n                        );\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            // Walked every server, none had a non-empty body.\n            let _ = tx.send(HoverOutcome::NoBody {\n                servers_tried: tried,\n            });\n        });\n    }\n\n    /// Drain the channel populated by [`Self::do_lsp_hover_request`]\n    /// and act on every pending [`HoverOutcome`]: open the popup\n    /// for `Body`, echo a clear message for `NoBody` / `NoServers`\n    /// so the user always knows their `K` press was processed.\n    /// Called once per main_loop iteration before draw; cheap\n    /// when the channel is empty (the common case).\n    pub fn drain_pending_hover(&mut self) {\n        let Some(mut rx) = self.pending_hover_rx.take() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        // Last-writer-wins -- if a stale outcome and a fresh one\n        // both queued (e.g. user pressed K twice quickly and\n        // both relays raced), we surface the latest.\n        let mut latest: Option<HoverOutcome> = None;\n        while let Ok(outcome) = rx.try_recv() {\n            latest = Some(outcome);\n        }\n        if let Some(outcome) = latest {\n            match outcome {\n                HoverOutcome::Body(body) => {\n                    self.do_open_hover(&body);\n                }\n                HoverOutcome::NoBody { servers_tried } => {\n                    self.set_message(\n                        EchoLevel::Info,\n                        format!(\n                            \"no hover info at cursor ({servers_tried} server{} replied)\",\n                            if servers_tried == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }\n                        ),\n                    );\n                }\n                HoverOutcome::NoServers => {\n                    self.set_message(\n                        EchoLevel::Warn,\n                        \"hover: no LSP servers attached for this buffer (\\\n                         check :lsp-status / :lsp-log)\"\n                            .to_string(),\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n            // Outcome delivered: clear the in-flight token so a\n            // subsequent motion doesn't try to flip a stale token.\n            self.pending_hover_token = None;\n        }\n        self.pending_hover_rx = Some(rx);\n    }\n\n    /// `gd` (Phase 4.2.c). Send `textDocument/definition` to every\n    /// LSP server attached to the active document. Same async\n    /// shape as [`Self::do_lsp_hover_request`]: spawn on the LSP\n    /// Generic dispatch for the four navigation flavours\n    /// (definition / declaration / typeDefinition / implementation\n    /// -- DESIGN.md §5.4 / docs/lsp-features.md). All share the\n    /// same `Vec<Location>` shape, so dispatch is parameterised\n    /// by [`LspNavKind`]; the kind selects the LSP method and\n    /// drives the user-facing echo from\n    /// [`Self::drain_pending_definitions`].\n    ///\n    /// Spawn the per-server walk on the LSP runtime, route the\n    /// merged + deduped location list back via\n    /// [`Self::pending_definition_rx`], drain on next frame.\n    ///\n    /// **Multi-server merge**: every server's response is\n    /// flattened to `Vec<Location>` (the lsp-types enum carries\n    /// `Scalar` / `Array` / `Link` shapes); the union is\n    /// deduplicated by `(uri, range.start)`. A single result\n    /// jumps; multiple results today echo a count and jump to the\n    /// first (the picker buffer lands with 4.2.d's references\n    /// view -- same shape, no point building it twice).\n    fn do_lsp_nav_request(&mut self, kind: LspNavKind) {\n        if let Some(token) = self.pending_definition_token.take() {\n            token.cancel();\n        }\n        let Some(uri) = self\n            .buffer_uris\n            .get(&self.document_buffer_id)\n            .cloned()\n        else {\n            self.set_message(\n                EchoLevel::Info,\n                \"no LSP server attached to current buffer\".to_string(),\n            );\n            return;\n        };\n        let snapshot = self.document.snapshot();\n        let lsp_position = match app_to_lsp_position(&snapshot.buffer, self.cursor) {\n            Some(p) => p,\n            None => {\n                self.set_message(\n                    EchoLevel::Error,\n                    format!(\"{}: cursor out of buffer\", kind.noun_singular()),\n                );\n                return;\n            }\n        };\n        // Capture the pre-jump origin for the tag stack -- the\n        // `gd` family is \"drill down\" navigation, so users\n        // expect `<C-t>` to walk back even after navigating\n        // through a chain of definitions. Keeps the active\n        // pane's buffer kind so dismissing into a help buffer\n        // pops correctly. The origin is consumed by the eventual\n        // jump (single-result drain or picker accept) and\n        // cleared otherwise.\n        let label = word_under_cursor(&snapshot.buffer, self.cursor).unwrap_or_default();\n        self.pending_tag_origin = Some(TagStackEntry {\n            buffer: self.active_buffer,\n            buffer_id: self.active_pane_buffer_id(),\n            position: self.cursor,\n            label,\n        });\n        let (tx, rx) =\n            tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<Vec<lsp_types::Location>>();\n        let token = lattice_protocol::CancellationToken::new();\n        self.pending_definition_rx = Some(rx);\n        self.pending_definition_token = Some(token.clone());\n        self.pending_nav_kind = Some(kind);\n        let lsp = self.lsp.clone();\n        crate::runtime::spawn_on_lsp_runtime(async move {\n            let handles: Vec<lattice_lsp::ServerHandle> =\n                { lsp.lock().await.servers_for(&uri) };\n            let mut all: Vec<lsp_types::Location> = Vec::new();\n            for handle in handles {\n                if token.is_cancelled() {\n                    return;\n                }\n                let pos_params = lsp_types::TextDocumentPositionParams {\n                    text_document: lsp_types::TextDocumentIdentifier { uri: uri.clone() },\n                    position: lsp_position,\n                };\n                let resp_locs = match kind {\n                    LspNavKind::Definition => {\n                        let params = lsp_types::GotoDefinitionParams {\n                            text_document_position_params: pos_params,\n                            work_done_progress_params: Default::default(),\n                            partial_result_params: Default::default(),\n                        };\n                        handle\n                            .goto_definition(params, token.clone())\n                            .await\n                            .ok()\n                            .flatten()\n                            .map(definition_response_to_locations)\n                            .unwrap_or_default()\n                    }\n                    LspNavKind::Declaration => {\n                        let params = lsp_types::request::GotoDeclarationParams {\n                            text_document_position_params: pos_params,\n                            work_done_progress_params: Default::default(),\n                            partial_result_params: Default::default(),\n                        };\n                        handle\n                            .goto_declaration(params, token.clone())\n                            .await\n                            .ok()\n                            .flatten()\n                            .map(definition_response_to_locations)\n                            .unwrap_or_default()\n                    }\n                    LspNavKind::TypeDefinition => {\n                        let params = lsp_types::request::GotoTypeDefinitionParams {\n                            text_document_position_params: pos_params,\n                            work_done_progress_params: Default::default(),\n                            partial_result_params: Default::default(),\n                        };\n                        handle\n                            .goto_type_definition(params, token.clone())\n                            .await\n                            .ok()\n                            .flatten()\n                            .map(definition_response_to_locations)\n                            .unwrap_or_default()\n                    }\n                    LspNavKind::Implementation => {\n                        let params = lsp_types::request::GotoImplementationParams {\n                            text_document_position_params: pos_params,\n                            work_done_progress_params: Default::default(),\n                            partial_result_params: Default::default(),\n                        };\n                        handle\n                            .goto_implementation(params, token.clone())\n                            .await\n                            .ok()\n                            .flatten()\n                            .map(definition_response_to_locations)\n                            .unwrap_or_default()\n                    }\n                };\n                all.extend(resp_locs);\n            }\n            // Dedup by (uri, range.start). Some servers emit the\n            // same location for overloaded methods; the picker\n            // shouldn't show duplicates.\n            all.sort_by(|a, b| {\n                let au = a.uri.as_str();\n                let bu = b.uri.as_str();\n                au.cmp(bu)\n                    .then_with(|| a.range.start.line.cmp(&b.range.start.line))\n                    .then_with(|| a.range.start.character.cmp(&b.range.start.character))\n            });\n            all.dedup_by(|a, b| {\n                a.uri.as_str() == b.uri.as_str() && a.range.start == b.range.start\n            });\n            let _ = tx.send(all);\n        });\n    }\n\n    /// Backwards-compat wrapper. Tests + plugin contributions may\n    /// reach for the named `do_lsp_definition_request`; this\n    /// keeps the public surface intact while the unified\n    /// [`Self::do_lsp_nav_request`] handles the actual work.\n    pub fn do_lsp_definition_request(&mut self) {\n        self.do_lsp_nav_request(LspNavKind::Definition)\n    }\n\n    /// Drain queued nav (definition / declaration / typeDef /\n    /// impl) results and act on them: 0 → echo, 1 → jump, N>1 →\n    /// echo count + jump to first (picker is 4.2.d's job; we\n    /// share its buffer surface rather than build two of them).\n    /// Pushes the pre-jump cursor onto the position history so\n    /// `<C-o>` walks back. The verb in echoes (`definitions` vs.\n    /// `implementations` etc.) reads from\n    /// [`Self::pending_nav_kind`].\n    pub fn drain_pending_definitions(&mut self) {\n        let Some(mut rx) = self.pending_definition_rx.take() else {\n            return;\n        };\n        let mut latest: Option<Vec<lsp_types::Location>> = None;\n        while let Ok(locs) = rx.try_recv() {\n            latest = Some(locs);\n        }\n        self.pending_definition_rx = Some(rx);\n        let locs = match latest {\n            Some(l) => l,\n            None => return,\n        };\n        // Result delivered; clear the in-flight token.\n        self.pending_definition_token = None;\n        let kind = self.pending_nav_kind.take().unwrap_or(LspNavKind::Definition);\n        let noun = kind.noun_plural();\n\n        match locs.len() {\n            0 => {\n                // No drill-down happened; drop the captured\n                // tag origin so a follow-up nav doesn't see a\n                // stale value.\n                self.pending_tag_origin = None;\n                self.set_message(EchoLevel::Info, format!(\"no {noun} found\"));\n            }\n            1 => {\n                // Vim-style \"do what I mean\" -- a single-result\n                // nav request still jumps directly. Multi-result\n                // opens the picker below. Single-result jump\n                // pushes the tag stack now.\n                if let Some(origin) = self.pending_tag_origin.take() {\n                    self.tag_stack.push(origin);\n                }\n                self.jump_to_lsp_location(&locs[0]);\n            }\n            _ => {\n                // Multi-result -- the picker will consume the\n                // pending tag origin on accept (see\n                // `do_picker_accept`); leave it set.\n                self.open_lsp_locations_picker(format!(\"lsp:{noun}\"), &locs);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// `gr` (Phase 4.2.d). Send `textDocument/references` to\n    /// every attached LSP server with `include_declaration: true`\n    /// (vim convention -- `gr` includes the symbol's own\n    /// declaration in the list). Spawn the per-server walk on\n    /// the LSP runtime; drain on the next frame opens a buffer-\n    /// backed `*lsp:references*` view in the active pane.\n    ///\n    /// Multi-server merge: union, deduped by `(uri, range.start)`.\n    fn do_lsp_references_request(&mut self) {\n        if let Some(token) = self.pending_references_token.take() {\n            token.cancel();\n        }\n        // Browse-style; not a tag-intent drill-down.\n        self.pending_tag_origin = None;\n        let Some(uri) = self\n            .buffer_uris\n            .get(&self.document_buffer_id)\n            .cloned()\n        else {\n            self.set_message(\n                EchoLevel::Info,\n                \"no LSP server attached to current buffer\".to_string(),\n            );\n            return;\n        };\n        let snapshot = self.document.snapshot();\n        let lsp_position = match app_to_lsp_position(&snapshot.buffer, self.cursor) {\n            Some(p) => p,\n            None => {\n                self.set_message(\n                    EchoLevel::Error,\n                    \"references: cursor out of buffer\".to_string(),\n                );\n                return;\n            }\n        };\n        // Capture the symbol under cursor for the help-buffer's\n        // title. Best-effort -- empty string when the cursor isn't\n        // on a word (the title falls back to \"(symbol)\").\n        let symbol = word_under_cursor(&snapshot.buffer, self.cursor).unwrap_or_default();\n        let (tx, rx) =\n            tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<ReferencesOutcome>();\n        let token = lattice_protocol::CancellationToken::new();\n        self.pending_references_rx = Some(rx);\n        self.pending_references_token = Some(token.clone());\n        let lsp = self.lsp.clone();\n        crate::runtime::spawn_on_lsp_runtime(async move {\n            let handles: Vec<lattice_lsp::ServerHandle> =\n                { lsp.lock().await.servers_for(&uri) };\n            if handles.is_empty() {\n                let _ =
